# Title: Mozilla Blocker - Block all Mozilla/Firefox Spying
# Description: Regular version - This blocklist aims to block almost all traffic to "Mozilla: Not a Browser/Tech Company - A Political, AI, Data Collection and Advertisement Organization". Mozilla accounts are blocked, mozilla download cdn is blocked. Use forks, otherwise get firefox from trusted sources like trusted stores and package repositories.
# Homepage: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Mozilla-Blocker
# Source: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Mozilla-Blocker/raw/branch/main/domains.txt
# License: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Mozilla-Blocker/src/branch/main/LICENSE
# Expires: 1 day
# Last modified: 10 October 2026 14:26:44 UTC
# Version: 4.90
# Syntax: domains (including possible subdomains)
# Number of entries: 8577
